You need to know which fix worked.<\/li>\n <\/ul>\n <\/div>\n <div class=\"monitor-card\">\n <span class=\"monitor-label\">Week 4<\/span>\n <div class=\"monitor-title\">30-Day Report to Champion<\/div>\n <ul class=\"monitor-items\">\n <li>Total volume handled vs. baseline estimate<\/li>\n <li>Actual resolution rate vs. SLO target<\/li>\n <li>Actual cost per run vs. business case estimate<\/li>\n <li>Top 3 failure modes identified and status (fixed \/ in progress \/ accepted risk)<\/li>\n <li>Recommendation: proceed to scale or extend Level 1 stabilization<\/li>\n <li>This report is Phase Gate 4 input. No scale decision without it.<\/li>\n <\/ul>\n <\/div>\n <\/div>\n\n <h3 class=\"subsection\">Drift and Degradation: When to Worry<\/h3>\n <p class=\"body-text\">Agents degrade over time even when nothing in the agent changes. Source data drifts, user input patterns shift, and the real-world distribution diverges from the test distribution. Here is what to watch for and what to do about it.<\/p>\n\n <table class=\"drift-table\" style=\"margin-top:24px;\">\n <thead><tr><th>Warning Sign<\/th><th>Severity<\/th><th>Likely Cause<\/th><th>First Response<\/th><\/tr><\/thead>\n <tbody>\n <tr><td>Error rate up 25\u201350% from baseline<\/td><td><span class=\"badge-warn\">WATCH<\/span><\/td><td>Input pattern shift, prompt edge case surfacing<\/td><td>Review last 20 failed cases. Identify common pattern. Update prompt.<\/td><\/tr>\n <tr><td>Error rate up 2x or more from baseline<\/td><td><span class=\"badge-crit\">ACT NOW<\/span><\/td><td>Integration failure, source data change, model behavior shift<\/td><td>Pause agent. Check integrations. Check source data schema. Roll back last prompt change.<\/td><\/tr>\n <tr><td>Latency up 50% with no volume change<\/td><td><span class=\"badge-warn\">WATCH<\/span><\/td><td>Provider API slowdown, tool timeout, context window growth<\/td><td>Check provider status. Check tool response times. Check if prompt has grown.<\/td><\/tr>\n <tr><td>Escalation rate trending steadily upward<\/td><td><span class=\"badge-warn\">WATCH<\/span><\/td><td>New input types not covered by training distribution<\/td><td>Categorize escalation reasons. Add handling for top 2 uncovered cases.<\/td><\/tr>\n <tr><td>Cost per run trending upward unexpectedly<\/td><td><span class=\"badge-warn\">WATCH<\/span><\/td><td>Input length growth, prompt chain getting longer, retry rate increasing<\/td><td>Check average input token count. Check retry logs. Check if any tool is failing and triggering retries.<\/td><\/tr>\n <tr><td>Integration auth failure<\/td><td><span class=\"badge-crit\">ACT NOW<\/span><\/td><td>Token expiry, API key rotation, endpoint change<\/td><td>Check auth token expiry. Rotate and update. Check API changelog for endpoint changes.<\/td><\/tr>\n <\/tbody>\n <\/table>\n <\/div>\n<\/section>\n\n<!-- PHASE 5 SCALE -->\n<section class=\"section-cream\" id=\"scale\">\n <div class=\"section-inner-wide\">\n <div class=\"phase-badge\" style=\"background:var(--bg-dark);\"><span class=\"phase-badge-dot\"><\/span> Phase 05 \u00b7 Scale<\/div>\n <h2 class=\"section-title\">When to Expand:<br>Scale Signals and Level 2 Readiness<\/h2>\n <p class=\"body-text\">Scaling before Level 1 proves value is not ambition. It is risk amplification. Multi-agent orchestration inherits every governance gap from Level 1 and multiplies it. The scale signals below are your unlock criteria. None of them are optional.<\/p>\n\n <table class=\"signal-table\" style=\"margin-top:32px;\">\n <thead><tr><th>Signal<\/th><th>Requirement<\/th><th>Verified By<\/th><\/tr><\/thead>\n <tbody>\n <tr><td>Production stability<\/td><td>30+ days in production without a P1 incident<\/td><td>Champion sign-off<\/td><\/tr>\n <tr><td>SLO performance<\/td><td>Error rate below threshold for 2 consecutive weeks<\/td><td>Monitoring dashboard review<\/td><\/tr>\n <tr><td>Cost predictability<\/td><td>Per-run cost within 15% of estimate for 4 consecutive weeks<\/td><td>Finance sign-off<\/td><\/tr>\n <tr><td>Volume proof<\/td><td>Agent handled planned volume target at planned accuracy<\/td><td>Ops lead sign-off<\/td><\/tr>\n <tr><td>Next use case identified<\/td><td>Level 2 use case scoped and champion named<\/td><td>Project lead sign-off<\/td><\/tr>\n <\/tbody>\n <\/table>\n\n <h3 class=\"subsection\">What the Three Levels Actually Mean<\/h3>\n <div class=\"levels-grid\">\n <div class=\"level-card\">\n <div class=\"level-num\">1<\/div>\n <div class=\"level-badge\">Level 1: Start Here<\/div>\n <div class=\"level-attr\"><span class=\"level-attr-label\">Architecture<\/span><span class=\"level-attr-val\">Single agent<\/span><\/div>\n <div class=\"level-attr\"><span class=\"level-attr-label\">Characteristics<\/span><span class=\"level-attr-val\">One trigger, one flow, defined SLOs, human oversight high<\/span><\/div>\n <div class=\"level-verdict\">Build this first. Prove value. Then earn Level 2.<\/div>\n <\/div>\n <div class=\"level-card\">\n <div class=\"level-num\">2<\/div>\n <div class=\"level-badge\">Level 2: After Scale Signals<\/div>\n <div class=\"level-attr\"><span class=\"level-attr-label\">Architecture<\/span><span class=\"level-attr-val\">Manager + specialist agents<\/span><\/div>\n <div class=\"level-attr\"><span class=\"level-attr-label\">Characteristics<\/span><span class=\"level-attr-val\">Parallel execution, cross-agent handoffs, governance layer critical<\/span><\/div>\n <div class=\"level-verdict\">Only after all scale signals confirmed. New governance review required.<\/div>\n <\/div>\n <div class=\"level-card\">\n <div class=\"level-num\">3<\/div>\n <div class=\"level-badge\">Level 3: Enterprise Only<\/div>\n <div class=\"level-attr\"><span class=\"level-attr-label\">Architecture<\/span><span class=\"level-attr-val\">Cross-functional agent networks<\/span><\/div>\n <div class=\"level-attr\"><span class=\"level-attr-label\">Characteristics<\/span><span class=\"level-attr-val\">Agents across departments, shared memory + knowledge graph<\/span><\/div>\n <div class=\"level-verdict\">Enterprise-only. Requires dedicated AI ops function.<\/div>\n <\/div>\n <\/div>\n <\/div>\n<\/section>\n\n<!-- 90-DAY GRID -->\n<section class=\"section-cream-alt\" id=\"grid\">\n <div class=\"section-inner-wide\">\n <span class=\"eyebrow-dark\">Operating Plan<\/span>\n <h2 class=\"section-title\">Your 90-Day Roadmap Grid<\/h2>\n <p class=\"body-text\">Fill in your specific use case, champion name, and target dates. Each phase gate must be signed before the next phase begins. Amber rows are Phase Gates: no phase begins without the gate signed.<\/p>\n\n <table class=\"roadmap-table\">\n opens until every box is checked and signed by both the project lead and the executive champion.
